11:T51f,;(function () {
  try {
    var t = localStorage.getItem('theme')
    if (t === 'light' || t === 'dark') {
      document.documentElement.classList.add(t)
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', t)
    } else if (t === 'system' && typeof window.matchMedia === 'function') {
      var v = window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches ? 'dark' : 'light'
      document.documentElement.classList.add(v)
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', v)
    } else {
      document.documentElement.classList.add('dark')
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark')
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  try {
    if (localStorage.getItem('sidebar-collapsed') === 'true') {
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-sidebar-collapsed', 'true')
    }
  } catch (e) {}
  // Reading mode: apply before paint so there is no flash even on full-page reloads.
  // The background inline style mirrors what ReadingModeContext sets via JS.
  // We read the CSS variable directly because the theme class is already on <html>.
  try {
    if (localStorage.getItem('reading-mode') === 'true') {
      document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-reading-mode', 'true')
      document.documentElement.style.background = 'hsl(var(--background))'
    }
  } catch (e) {}

          children: "置信度（confidence）"
        }), "\n模型在检测过程中，一个目标上可能会出现多个框，每一个框会对应一个 confidence，confidence 阈值就是之后计算 TP，FP，TN，FN 等参数的界限；\n", _jsx(_components.strong, {
          children: "交并比（IOU）"
        }), "\n计算模型预测出的框和标签中标注的框的 IOU，IOU 用于判定目标是真目标还是假目标，大于 IOU 阈值的框会认定为 True，小于 IOU 阈值的检测框会认定为 False；\n计算 TP，FP，TN，FN"]
      }), "\n", _jsxs(_components.ul, {
        children: ["\n", _jsx(_components.li, {
          children: "TP：大于 IOU 阈值且大于 confidence 阈值（实际是正样本，判定为正样本）；（检测对了）"
        }), "\n", _jsx(_components.li, {
          children: "FP：小于 IOU 阈值且大于 confidence 阈值（实际是负样本，判定为正样本）；（检测错了）"
        }), "\n", _jsx(_components.li, {
          children: "TN：小于 IOU 阈值且小于 confidence 阈值（实际是负样本，判定为负样本）；（不用于计算）"
        }), "\n", _jsx(_components.li, {
          children: "FN：大于 IOU 阈值且小于 confidence 阈值（实际是正样本，判定为负样本）；（没检测出来）"
        }), "\n"]
      }), "\n"]
    }), "\n", _jsxs(_components.ul, {
      children: ["\n", _jsxs(_components.li, {
        children: ["\n", _jsx(_components.p, {

          children: ["\n", _jsxs(_components.li, {
            children: ["基于 IoU 计算（预测区域和标记区域的交并集）", "\n", _jsxs(_components.ul, {
              children: ["\n", _jsx(_components.li, {
                children: "Precision: TP / (TP + FP) 查准率"

function MDXContent(props = {}) {
  const {wrapper: MDXLayout} = {
    ..._provideComponents(),
    ...props.components
  };
  return MDXLayout ? _jsx(MDXLayout, {
    ...props,
    children: _jsx(_createMdxContent, {
      ...props
    })
  }) : _createMdxContent(props);
}
return {
  default: MDXContent
};
